BIM-context impact — name-only vs +BIM context

Query enriched with name + class + description + concrete grade + struct class (the universal best combo). The BM25F leg always ranks on the bare name, and Regex (a pattern mode) is never enriched. Fuzzy is shown enriched too — not because it helps (it does not), but to document comprehensively that the lexical path degrades under enrichment, leaving no open question.

Methodology

  • Recall@K (acceptable set): fraction of ground-truth-match queries whose first acceptable UUID lands within the top-K returned candidates. R@All counts a hit anywhere in the full ranked list.
  • Recall@K (exact correct): same, but a hit means the row's UUID equals the canonical correct_uuid. Distinguishes "the right EPD" from "any acceptable EPD" — the reranker's job is most visible here.
  • Wilson 95% CI on every R@K cell: binomial confidence interval after Wilson 1927. Far less degenerate than the normal approximation at small N or near 0%/100%.
  • MRR (mean reciprocal rank): mean of 1/rank of the first hit per query (0 if missed). Reported separately for the acceptable set and the correct-only hit.
  • Bootstrap CI on MRR: 1000-replicate percentile interval (Efron & Tibshirani 1993; seed = 42 for reproducibility).
  • McNemar exact (1947): two-sided binomial p-value on the discordant pairs of paired hit/miss vectors. Computed for every mode-pair at R@1 and R@10. Exact (not chi-squared) — stays valid on the small ground-truth sizes typical of construction LCA.
  • Denominator: only queries whose GT entry has label: "match" are counted. Entries labeled skip or with no GT match are still shown in the per-query details but excluded from every aggregate.
  • Acceptable set: union of correct_uuid and acceptable_uuids from each GT entry. The correct_uuid is highlighted in the per-query tables with a double star (★★).
  • Search backends: Regex (template scoring) and Fuzzy (3-tier Levenshtein) — both BM25F-ranked (Robertson & Spärck-Jones 1976; Zaragoza et al. 2004). Semantic (cosine over bge-m3 embeddings via Ollama). Hybrid (Reciprocal Rank Fusion of BM25F + cosine, k=60, Cormack et al. 2009). Hybrid+Rerank (top-K from Hybrid passed through BAAI/bge-reranker-v2-m3 cross-encoder for joint-attention re-scoring). All modes share the same calibrated Score% via ConfidenceCalibrator (MAX_CONFIDENCE = 0.97 cap).

Ground-truth construction (from GT JSON):

Jedes der 88 BIM-Materialien wurde gegen die explizite Systemgrenze als MAP oder SKIP beurteilt, ausschliesslich aus dem lokalen BIM-Kontext und dem 2341-Datensatz-Cache a2_sphera DE. Kandidatenpools wurden deterministisch nach OEKOBAUDAT-Klassifikationsfamilie aufgezaehlt (taxonomie-getrieben, unabhaengig von der BM25F/semantischen Suche des Plugins) und von einem LCA/AEC-Materialspezialisten gerankt. correct_uuid = das namens-/funktionsnaechste EPD (generisch/repraesentativ bevorzugt, R5); acceptable_uuids = alle echten funktionalen Substitute in Rangfolge (R2/R3), Note 3=exakt, 2=stark, 1=lockerer. Nahezu identische Familien (z. B. werksspezifischer Transportbeton der passenden Festigkeitsklasse, alle Mineralwolle-Varianten) sind vollstaendig gelistet (R9). Alle UUID/Name/Klassifikation wurden zur Assemblierzeit byte-genau per UUID aus dem DE-Cache gezogen.

  • Rang 1 = namens-/funktionsnaechstes EPD; Relevanz 3.
  • acceptable_uuids = correct zuerst, dann alle echten Substitute nach Note und Naehe.
  • Generische / repraesentative / Durchschnitts-EPDs vor Einzelhersteller-EPDs fuer Rang 1 (R5); deutscher Name moeglichst direkt passend.
  • Tie-breaker: Beton-Festigkeitsklasse (MPa->C-Klasse), Rohdichte, Waermeleitfaehigkeit, Bauteilfunktion, Steifigkeit (hart->Hartschaum, weich->Matte).
  • Funktionale Aequivalenz entscheidet ueber Akzeptanz: Kandidaten duerfen Klassifikationsblaetter nur kreuzen, wenn ein LCA-Pruefer sie als austauschbar ansieht; nie ueber Funktion/Form hinweg (R3, Kreuzfamilie <= Note 2).
